All cloud resources and IAM policies must be declared in Terraform/OpenTofu (`aws_vpc`, `aws_subnet`, `aws_internet_gateway`, `aws_route_table`, `aws_security_group`, `aws_lb`, `aws_lb_target_group`, `aws_lb_listener`, `aws_ecs_cluster`, `aws_ecs_task_definition`, `aws_ecs_service`, `aws_db_subnet_group`, `aws_db_instance`, `aws_dynamodb_table`, `aws_elasticache_subnet_group`, `aws_elasticache_replication_group`, `aws_s3_bucket`, `aws_s3_bucket_versioning`, `aws_s3_bucket_server_side_encryption_configuration`, `aws_s3_bucket_public_access_block`, `aws_sqs_queue`, `aws_lambda_function`, `aws_lambda_event_source_mapping`, `aws_scheduler_schedule`, `aws_cognito_user_pool`, `aws_cognito_resource_server`, `aws_cognito_user_pool_client`, `aws_iam_role`, `aws_iam_role_policy`, `aws_kms_key`, `aws_kms_alias`, `aws_cloudwatch_log_group`) rather than created via imperative `aws` CLI commands in `deploy.sh`.\n\n## Service Specifications\n\nRead the individual service contracts in `/workspace/contracts/services/` for the exact resource attributes and wiring requirements:\n\n- [VPC and Security Groups](services/vpc.md)\n- [Application Load Balancer](services/alb.md)\n- [ECS Fargate Service](services/ecs.md)\n- [RDS PostgreSQL](services/rds.md)\n- [SQS Main Queue and DLQ](services/sqs.md)\n- [Lambda Workers and Event Source Mapping](services/lambda.md)\n- [DynamoDB Projection Store](services/dynamodb.md)\n- [ElastiCache for Valkey](services/elasticache-valkey.md)\n- [S3 Audit Archive](services/s3.md)\n- [EventBridge Scheduler](services/scheduler.md)\n- [Cognito User Pool and OAuth2 Scopes](services/cognito.md)\n- [IAM Roles and Least-Privilege Policies](services/iam.md)\n- [Customer-Managed KMS Keys](services/kms.md)\n- [CloudWatch Log Groups](services/cloudwatch-logs.md)\n\n## Submission Scripts and Manifest\n\n1. `/workspace/submission/deploy.sh`:\n   - Must be executable and idempotent (timeout: 720s).\n   - Runs `terraform` or `tofu` against `/workspace/submission/infra` with state saved at `/workspace/submission/infra/terraform.tfstate`.\n   - Idempotently initializes the `clearledger` PostgreSQL schema, tables, relational constraints, triggers, and indexes on the RDS instance as specified in [RDS PostgreSQL](services/rds.md).\n   - Writes `/workspace/submission/manifest.json` (max 1 MiB) conforming strictly to `/workspace/contracts/schemas/manifest.schema.json`.\n   - Polls `GET <service_url>/health/ready` until the API returns HTTP `200`.\n   - Re-running `deploy.sh` must preserve the RDS instance, DynamoDB table, and S3 bucket without data loss, reconcile control-plane drift across all managed AWS resources (including SQS/DLQ attributes, EventBridge Scheduler schedules, CloudWatch Log Group retention, Lambda worker environment variables, out-of-band inline or attached IAM policies on the six workload roles, and security group egress rules), recreate any missing resources or event source mappings, and converge all derived data stores (DynamoDB, Valkey, and the versioned S3 audit bucket including purging noncurrent object `Versions` and `DeleteMarkers`) 1-to-1 with authoritative PostgreSQL state before exiting `0`.\n2. `/workspace/submission/destroy.sh`:\n   - Must be executable (timeout: 900s) and cleanly destroy all resources created for `resource_prefix` while leaving all pre-existing baseline (`cl-base-*`) resources untouched.\n   - Must handle non-empty versioned S3 buckets, attached or multi-version IAM policies, and any out-of-band resources scoped to `<resource_prefix>` (by name prefix or `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` tag) so that post-destroy inventory matches the pre-deployment baseline.\n\n# ClearLedger Runtime and Application Contract\n\nAll four application images are pre-built and loaded into the local container daemon. Do not rebuild or modify the application binaries. Configure each runtime using the environment variables below.\n\n## 1. API Service (`api_image` on ECS Fargate)\n\n- **Binary**: `/usr/local/bin/clearledger-api`\n- **Listening port**: `8080` (`PORT=8080`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `PORT`: `\"8080\"`\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json` (`us-east-1`)\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json` (`us-east-1`)\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: control-plane endpoint reachable from the container (`http://aws:4566`)\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: PostgreSQL connection string `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `SQS_QUEUE_URL`: URL of the main SQS event queue\n  - `PROJECTION_TABLE`: name of the DynamoDB projection table\n  - `VALKEY_URL`: Valkey connection URL `redis://<valkey_host>:<valkey_port>`\n  - `CACHE_TTL_SECONDS`: `\"90\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"90\"`)\n  - `AUTH_ISSUER`: Cognito issuer URL (`http://aws:4566/<user_pool_id>`)\n  - `AUTH_AUDIENCES`: comma-separated Cognito client IDs (`<read_client_id>,<write_client_id>,<admin_client_id>`)\n  - `AUTH_JWKS_URL`: Cognito JWKS URL (`http://aws:4566/<user_pool_id>/.well-known/jwks.json`)\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the API CloudWatch Log Group\n  - `SERVICE_INSTANCE_ID` (optional): instance identifier returned in `X-ClearLedger-Instance` (defaults to `HOSTNAME`)\n\nUpon startup, `clearledger-api` connects to PostgreSQL and serves HTTP traffic on `0.0.0.0:8080`. The application binaries do **not** run database migrations automatically: `deploy.sh` must idempotently apply the `clearledger` PostgreSQL schema (`clearledger.settlements`, `clearledger.events`, `clearledger.outbox`, `clearledger.idempotency_keys`), all required relational `CHECK` / `UNIQUE` / `FOREIGN KEY` constraints, all state-transition, cross-table, and append-only triggers across all four tables, and its three indexes as defined in `services/rds.md` before `GET /health/ready` reports `200 OK` (`checks.postgres = \"UP\"`). During `POST /v1/settlements` and `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`, `clearledger-api` maps PostgreSQL constraint and trigger exceptions (`SQLSTATE 23514`, `23503`, `23502`, `23505`, and `P0001`) to HTTP `400 Bad Request` (`invalid_settlement`).\n\n## 2. Projector Worker (`projector_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: SQS event source mapping from the main SQS queue (`batch_size = 5`, `maximum_batching_window_in_seconds = 0`, `function_response_types = [\"ReportBatchItemFailures\"]`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `PROJECTION_TABLE`: name of the DynamoDB projection table\n  - `VALKEY_URL`: `redis://<valkey_host>:<valkey_port>`\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the projector CloudWatch Log Group\n\nFor each valid event envelope in the SQS batch:\n- Inserts the event item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"EVENT#<8-digit-zero-padded-version>\"`) in DynamoDB using a conditional `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` write.\n- Updates the aggregate state item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"STATE\"`, `GSI1PK = \"ACCOUNT#<account_id>\"`, `GSI1SK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`) using a conditional write only when `aggregateVersion` is strictly greater than the stored `version`. Duplicate or older versions are ignored idempotently; if an older version (such as `v1`) arrives after a newer version, any missing static metadata (`reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`) is backfilled without regressing `version`, `status`, or `clearing_stage`.\n- Deletes the Valkey key `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>`.\n- If any record fails validation or deserialization, its `messageId` is returned in `batchItemFailures` so SQS retries only the failed message and routes poison messages to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4` receives.\n\n## 3. Outbox Relay Worker (`relay_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 1 minute (`rate(1 minute)`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `SQS_QUEUE_URL`: URL of the main SQS event queue\n  - `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE`: `\"50\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"50\"`)\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the relay CloudWatch Log Group\n\nQueries up to `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE` rows from `clearledger.outbox` where `published_at IS NULL` ordered by `seq ASC`, sends each event envelope to `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, and updates `published_at = NOW(), attempts = attempts + 1, last_error = NULL`.\n\n## 4. Audit Archiver Worker (`archiver_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 5 minutes (`rate(5 minutes)`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `AUDIT_BUCKET`: name of the S3 audit archive bucket\n  - `AUDIT_PREFIX`: `\"ledger-audit/\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"ledger-audit/\"`)\n  - `AUDIT_BATCH_SIZE` (optional): defaults to `100`\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the archiver CloudWatch Log Group\n\nSelects published outbox rows (`published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`) ordered by `seq ASC`, writes a newline-delimited JSON object to `s3://<AUDIT_BUCKET>/ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`, and sets `archived_at = NOW()`.\n\n## 5. OAuth2 Scopes and Authorization\n\nAll protected endpoints validate Bearer JWTs issued by the Cognito User Pool against `AUTH_ISSUER`, `AUTH_JWKS_URL`, and `AUTH_AUDIENCES`. Scopes are strictly non-hierarchical:\n\n- `clearledger/read`: permitted only on `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` and `GET /v1/settlements/{id}/ledger`.\n- `clearledger/write`: permitted only on `POST /v1/settlements` and `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`.\n- `clearledger/admin`: permitted only on `POST /v1/admin/projections/{id}/rebuild`.\n\nMissing or invalid tokens return `401 Unauthorized`; valid tokens lacking the exact endpoint scope return `403 Forbidden`.\n\n## 6. Recovery Drills, Bidirectional Data-Plane Reconciliation, and Lifecycle\n\n- **Backlog & DLQ**: When the projector event source mapping is disabled, API writes commit to PostgreSQL and enqueue on SQS while `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` returns `404` until re-enabled. Duplicate, out-of-order, and concurrent event deliveries are handled without version regression; invalid envelopes are retried and routed to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4`.\n- **Bidirectional Multi-Store Convergence in `deploy.sh`**:\n  - PostgreSQL (`clearledger.settlements`, `clearledger.events`, `clearledger.outbox`) is the authoritative system of record; DynamoDB, Valkey, and S3 are derived read/archive stores.\n  - Because `clearledger-projector` writes `EVENT#*` items conditionally with `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` and updates `STATE` conditionally only when `aggregateVersion > current_version`, and because `clearledger-audit-archiver` only archives rows where `published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`, `deploy.sh` must converge both the control plane and all derived data stores against PostgreSQL before exiting `0`:\n    - All unpublished outbox rows (`published_at IS NULL`) must be relayed to SQS.\n    - The DynamoDB projection table must contain **only** partitions for settlements that exist in `clearledger.settlements`, and each partition `PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"` must contain **only** `SK = \"STATE\"` and `SK = \"EVENT#00000001\" .. \"EVENT#<version:08d>\"` with every attribute (including `AccountIndex` `GSI1PK` / `GSI1SK`, `updated_at`, `last_entry_id`, `last_memo`, and each event's `entry_id`, `memo`, `occurred_at`, `correlation_id`, and `envelope`) matching PostgreSQL.\n    - Valkey must contain **only** `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>` keys for settlements that exist in `clearledger.settlements` (purging any orphan settlement keys as well as any stray keys outside the `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>` namespace), and every settlement in `clearledger.settlements` must be actively populated in Valkey at `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>` with a valid TTL in `(0, 90]` and a canonical `SettlementProjection` JSON payload matching the authoritative projection state (so `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` immediately after `deploy.sh` hits cache and returns `X-ClearLedger-Source: cache`).\n    - The S3 audit archive bucket (`s3://<AUDIT_BUCKET>`) must contain objects **only** under `ledger-audit/`, every object key and body must conform to `ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson` with a contiguous, gap-free slice of committed `clearledger.outbox` records in `[first_seq, last_seq]` ordered by strictly ascending `clearledger.outbox.seq` and strictly disjoint, non-overlapping `[first_seq, last_seq]` intervals across batch objects, the union of all S3 batch records must match `clearledger.outbox` **1-to-1** (every committed outbox event present in S3 **exactly once** with its exact canonical JSON `payload`, and `archived_at IS NOT NULL` in PostgreSQL), and all noncurrent object `Versions` (`IsLatest = false`) and `DeleteMarkers` (`ListObjectVersions` / `DeleteObject` with `VersionId`) must be permanently purged from the versioned bucket so only the single current version (`IsLatest = true`) of each canonical batch remains.\n    - Do not use a shared `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/*` or `/clearledger/*` log group wildcard that permits one workload to write into another workload's log group, and do not grant any CloudWatch Logs actions to `scheduler_role_arn`.\n- **Strict KMS key isolation across all six roles**: Each workload may only be granted `kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, and `kms:DescribeKey` on the specific customer-managed KMS keys required for the data stores it accesses at runtime. No role may grant `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on any of the other KMS keys, and `ecs_execution_role_arn` and `scheduler_role_arn` must not grant access to any of the four KMS keys.\n- **Defense-in-depth explicit `Deny` guardrail statements**: Do not rely solely on implicit omission for forbidden permissions. Every role's policy document must include explicit `Effect = \"Deny\"` statement(s) covering all forbidden actions and resources listed under **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)** for that role below.\n- **Out-of-band IAM policy drift reconciliation in `deploy.sh`**: During `deploy.sh`, any out-of-band inline policies (`DeleteRolePolicy`) or attached customer-managed/AWS-managed policies (`DetachRolePolicy`) added to any of the six ClearLedger IAM roles outside Terraform/OpenTofu must be removed so that each role retains only its canonical least-privilege policy, and any detached out-of-band customer-managed IAM policies prefixed with `<resource_prefix>` must also be deleted (`DeletePolicy`, after deleting any non-default policy versions).\n\n## Role Responsibilities and Access Boundaries\n\n1. **`ecs_execution_role_arn`**:\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on the audit bucket (`<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`), `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on all four KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on the three non-API CloudWatch Log Groups (`projector`, `relay`, `archiver`).\n2. **`ecs_task_role_arn`**:\n   - `engine = \"postgres\"`\n  - `engine_version = \"16.3\"` (or `16.*`)\n  - `instance_class = \"db.t4g.micro\"`\n  - `db_name`, `username`, and `password` from `/workspace/config/config.json`\n  - `storage_encrypted = true`\n  - `kms_key_id` set to the customer-managed `database` KMS key ARN (`kms.database_arn`)\n  - `publicly_accessible = false`\n  - `skip_final_snapshot = true`\n  - `vpc_security_group_ids` containing the `rds` security group\n- Re-running `deploy.sh` must never replace the RDS instance or lose committed data.\n\n## Required Database Schema, Relational Invariants, and Triggers\n\nThe pre-built Rust binaries (`clearledger-api`, `clearledger-outbox-relay`, and `clearledger-audit-archiver`) do **not** run database migrations on startup. `clearledger-api` relies on PostgreSQL `CHECK`/`UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY` constraints and PL/pgSQL triggers to enforce domain, schema, and state-transition invariants at the database layer (mapping SQLSTATE `23514`, `23503`, `23502`, `23505`, and `P0001` exceptions to HTTP `400 Bad Request`), and `GET /health/ready` verifies that all four tables, all three indexes, and user-defined triggers on all four tables exist in the `clearledger` schema before reporting `checks.postgres = \"UP\"`.\n\n`deploy.sh` must idempotently initialize the `clearledger` schema, all four tables, their constraints, their triggers, and all three indexes on the RDS PostgreSQL instance.\n\n### 1. Table `clearledger.settlements`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `account_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `reference TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `debit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `credit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_status TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_stage TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `last_entry_id UUID NULL`\n  - `last_memo TEXT NULL`\n  - `version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE UPDATE` trigger)**:\n  - Trimmed string length bounds and allowed status values must match `CreateSettlementRequest` and `AppendEntryRequest` in `openapi.yaml` (including `1..256` trimmed characters when `last_memo` is non-null), with trimmed `debit_party <> credit_party`, `version >= 1`, and monotonic timestamps (`updated_at >= created_at` on insert and `NEW.updated_at >= OLD.updated_at` on update).\n  - At initiation (`version = 1`), `entry_count = 0`, `current_status = 'INITIATED'`, `last_entry_id IS NULL`, and `updated_at = created_at`. For `version > 1`, `entry_count = version - 1`, `current_status <> 'INITIATED'`, and `last_entry_id IS NOT NULL`.\n  - On `UPDATE`: header columns (`settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `created_at`) are immutable; each update must step `version` and `entry_count` by `+1` with a new `last_entry_id` (`NEW.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id`); and `current_status` must follow the clearing lifecycle progression:\n    - The non-`DISPUTED` clearing status rank order is `INITIATED` (`0`) < `VALIDATED` (`1`) < `RESERVED` (`2`) < `CLEARED` (`3`) < `SETTLED` (`4`) < `RECONCILED` (`5`).\n    - `RECONCILED` is strictly terminal: once `OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`, any `UPDATE` on the settlement row must be rejected (even if `NEW.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`).\n    - From any non-`RECONCILED`, non-`DISPUTED` status (`INITIATED`, `VALIDATED`, `RESERVED`, `CLEARED`, `SETTLED`), `NEW.current_status` may transition to `'DISPUTED'` or to any status whose rank is monotonic non-decreasing (`rank(NEW.current_status) >= rank(OLD.current_status)` — permitting same-status updates such as `CLEARED` -> `CLEARED` and single- or multi-stage forward advances such as `INITIATED` -> `VALIDATED` or `INITIATED` -> `CLEARED`, while rejecting any backward rank regression where `rank(NEW.current_status) < rank(OLD.current_status)` such as `CLEARED` -> `RESERVED`).\n    - From `OLD.current_status = 'DISPUTED'`, `NEW.current_status` may only remain `'DISPUTED'` or resolve to terminal `'RECONCILED'`.\n\n### 2. Table `clearledger.events`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `event_type TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `occurred_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE INSERT`/`UPDATE`/`DELETE` triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `UNIQUE (settlement_id, idempotency_key)`, with trimmed length bounds on `correlation_id` and `idempotency_key` matching `openapi.yaml`.\n  - `payload` must conform strictly to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` in `schemas/events.schema.json` (including `additionalProperties: false` on both the envelope and `payload.data`, required non-null `accountId`, `reference`, `debitParty`, `creditParty`, `status`, and `clearingStage` in `payload.data` with trimmed string length bounds matching `openapi.yaml` and `1..256` trimmed characters when `data.memo` is non-null, column-to-envelope equality across `event_id`, `settlement_id`, `aggregate_version`, `event_type`, `correlation_id`, `idempotency_key`, and `occurred_at` compared as `TIMESTAMPTZ`, and version/kind/status/entryId coupling for `SettlementInitiated` at `v = 1` vs `LedgerEntryRecorded` at `v >= 2`).\n  - On `INSERT`: per-settlement `aggregate_version` must be contiguous starting at `1`; `data.entryId` must be unique per settlement across all `LedgerEntryRecorded` events; the event must match the parent row in `clearledger.settlements` (including header fields, `current_status`, `current_stage`, `last_entry_id`, `last_memo`, `version`, and `occurred_at = updated_at`, plus `occurred_at = created_at` at `v = 1`); and for `aggregate_version >= 2`, `NEW.occurred_at` must be `>=` the immediately preceding event's `occurred_at` (`aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1`) and the status transition from that preceding event's `data.status` to `NEW.payload->'data'->>'status'` must satisfy the settlement status transition rules above (monotonic non-decreasing rank, reachable `DISPUTED` from non-`RECONCILED`, `DISPUTED` only to `DISPUTED` or `RECONCILED`, and no transitions out of or repeating `RECONCILED`).\n  - `clearledger.events` is strictly append-only (`UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected).\n\n### 3. Table `clearledger.outbox`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES clearledger.events(event_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `published_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `archived_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `last_error TEXT NULL`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY`/`CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `FOREIGN KEY (settlement_id, aggregate_version) REFERENCES clearledger.events(settlement_id, aggregate_version) ON DELETE CASCADE`.\n  - `payload` must conform to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` (`schemas/events.schema.json`) and on `INSERT` must exactly mirror the corresponding row in `clearledger.events` in contiguous per-settlement `aggregate_version` order starting at `1`.\n  - Delivery/archival lifecycle: `attempts >= 0`; when `published_at IS NOT NULL`, `attempts >= 1`, `last_error IS NULL`, and `published_at >= created_at`; `archived_at` requires `published_at IS NOT NULL` and `archived_at >= published_at`.\n  - `DELETE` is forbidden; on `UPDATE`, envelope columns are immutable, `attempts` cannot decrease, publishing an unpublished row requires incrementing `attempts` with `archived_at IS NULL`, while `published_at` remains `NOT NULL` (such as during archival or resetting `archived_at = NULL` for S3 re-archival), `published_at`, `attempts`, and `last_error` cannot be mutated (resetting `published_at = NULL, archived_at = NULL` for operational replay is permitted), and once `OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL` and `NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL`, `NEW.archived_at` cannot be mutated to a different timestamp without first resetting `archived_at = NULL`.\n\n### 4. Table `clearledger.idempotency_keys`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `scope TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `request_hash TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `status_code INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `response_body JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - Composite `PRIMARY KEY (scope, idempotency_key)`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - `scope` (`create:<settlement_uuid>` or `entry:<settlement_uuid>`), `idempotency_key` (`8..128` trimmed chars), lowercase hex SHA-256 `request_hash`, `status_code` (`201` for `create:*` at `version = 1`; `202` for `entry:*` at `version >= 2`), and closed-schema `response_body` (`WriteAcceptedResponse` in `openapi.yaml` with `accepted = true` and `idempotentReplay = false`).\n  - On `INSERT`, the referenced event (`eventId`, `settlementId`, `version`, `idempotency_key`) must exist in both `clearledger.events` and `clearledger.outbox`. `UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected.\n\n### 5. Required Indexes in `clearledger` Schema\n\n- `idx_clearledger_outbox_unpublished`: on `clearledger.outbox (seq)` with partial predicate `WHERE published_at IS NULL`\n- `idx_clearledger_outbox_unarchived`: on `clearledger.outbox (seq)` with partial predicate `WHERE published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`\n- `idx_clearledger_events_settlement_version`: on `clearledger.events (settlement_id, aggregate_version)`\n=== s3.md\n# S3 Audit Archive (`services/s3.md`)\n\n- Provision a dedicated S3 bucket (`aws_s3_bucket`) for NDJSON audit batches (`force_destroy = true` recommended so `destroy.sh` can remove the bucket after versioned audit objects and delete markers are written).\n- Enable bucket versioning (`aws_s3_bucket_versioning` with `status = \"Enabled\"`).\n- Enable default server-side encryption (`aws_s3_bucket_server_side_encryption_configuration`) using `sse_algorithm = \"aws:kms\"` and `kms_master_key_id = kms.audit_arn`.\n- Block all public access (`aws_s3_bucket_public_access_block`) with all four flags (`block_public_acls`, `block_public_policy`, `ignore_public_acls`, `restrict_public_buckets`) set to `true`.\n- Configure the Audit Archiver Lambda with `AUDIT_BUCKET = <bucket_name>` and `AUDIT_PREFIX = \"ledger-audit/\"` (`manifest.audit.prefix = \"ledger-audit/\"`).\n- `clearledger-audit-archiver` writes NDJSON batches to `s3://<bucket_name>/ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`, where `<first_seq:08d>` and `<last_seq:08d>` are the 8-digit zero-padded first and last `clearledger.outbox.seq` in the batch, each batch contains a contiguous, gap-free slice of all committed `clearledger.outbox` rows whose `seq` lies in `[first_seq, last_seq]` ordered by strictly ascending `clearledger.outbox.seq`, batch `[first_seq, last_seq]` intervals are strictly disjoint and non-overlapping across objects, and `<16_char_sha256_hex>` is the first 16 lowercase hexadecimal characters of the SHA-256 digest of the object's raw bytes.\n- During `deploy.sh` data-plane convergence, reconcile the versioned S3 audit bucket against `clearledger.outbox` so that all objects reside under `ledger-audit/`, every batch object has a valid canonical key (`batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`), valid SHA-256 digest, contiguous gap-free `clearledger.outbox.seq` coverage across `[first_seq, last_seq]`, and non-overlapping `[first_seq, last_seq]` bounds across batches, every committed event in `clearledger.outbox` appears **exactly once** across the S3 audit batches with its exact canonical JSON `payload` and `archived_at IS NOT NULL` in PostgreSQL, and all noncurrent object `Versions` (`IsLatest = false`) and `DeleteMarkers` (`ListObjectVersions` / `DeleteObject` with `VersionId`) are permanently purged from the bucket so only the single current version (`IsLatest = true`) of each valid canonical batch remains.\n\n=== scheduler.md\n# EventBridge Scheduler (`services/scheduler.md`)\n\nProvision two enabled EventBridge Scheduler schedules (`aws_scheduler_schedule`, `state = \"ENABLED\"`, `flexible_time_window { mode = \"OFF\" }`):\n\n1. **Outbox Relay Schedule (`schedules.outbox_schedule_name`)**:\n   - `schedule_expression = \"rate(1 minute)\"`\n   - `target.arn = workers.outbox_relay.function_arn`\n   - `target.role_arn = iam.scheduler_role_arn`\n2. **Audit Archiver Schedule (`schedules.archive_schedule_name`)**:\n   - `schedule_expression = \"rate(5 minutes)\"`\n   - `target.arn = workers.audit_archiver.function_arn`\n   - `target.role_arn = iam.scheduler_role_arn`\n=== sqs.md\n# SQS Main Queue and Dead-Letter Queue (`services/sqs.md`)\n\n- Provision a dead-letter queue (`aws_sqs_queue`) encrypted with the customer-managed `messaging` KMS key (`kms_master_key_id = kms.messaging_arn`) and `message_retention_seconds = 1209600` (14 days).\n- Provision the main event queue (`aws_sqs_queue`) with:\n  - `kms_master_key_id = kms.messaging_arn`\n  - `visibility_timeout_seconds = 3`\n  - `receive_wait_time_seconds = 2`\n  - `message_retention_seconds = 172800` (2 days)\n  - Redrive policy (configured either inline via `redrive_policy` on `aws_sqs_queue` or via a standalone `aws_sqs_queue_redrive_policy` resource) routing failed messages to the DLQ with `maxReceiveCount = 4` (`manifest.messaging.max_receive_count = 4`).\n- If the main queue or its Lambda event source mapping is deleted during an outage drill, or if queue attributes (such as `VisibilityTimeout` on the main queue or `MessageRetentionPeriod` on the DLQ) drift out-of-band, re-running `deploy.sh` must reconcile or recreate the queue, re-bind its Lambda event source mapping cleanly, update `manifest.json`, and drain/reconcile any pending outbox events, projections, and audit archives before exiting `0`.\n=== vpc.md\n# VPC and Security Groups (`services/vpc.md`)\n\n- Provision one VPC (`aws_vpc`) with DNS support (`enable_dns_support = true`) and DNS hostnames (`enable_dns_hostnames = true`) enabled.\n- Create at least two public subnets (`aws_subnet`, `map_public_ip_on_launch = true`) across `us-east-1a` and `us-east-1b`.\n- Create at least two private subnets (`aws_subnet`, `map_public_ip_on_launch = false`) across `us-east-1a` and `us-east-1b`.\n- Attach an Internet Gateway (`aws_internet_gateway`) to the VPC and associate (`aws_route_table_association`) a public route table (`0.0.0.0/0` -> Internet Gateway) with both public subnets.\n- Associate (`aws_route_table_association`) a private route table (with no route to an Internet Gateway) with both private subnets.\n- Create four distinct dedicated security groups (`aws_security_group`, four distinct security group IDs) enforcing least-privilege ingress and egress:\n  - `alb`:\n    - **Ingress**: TCP port `80` (`from_port = 80`, `to_port = 80`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from `0.0.0.0/0`.\n    - **Egress**: restricted strictly to TCP port `8080` (`from_port = 8080`, `to_port = 8080`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) targeting the `ecs` security group or VPC CIDR (must **not** allow `0.0.0.0/0`, `::/0`, or `protocol = \"-1\"` on egress).\n  - `ecs`:\n    - **Ingress**: restricted strictly to TCP port `8080` (`from_port = 8080`, `to_port = 8080`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from the `alb` security group ID (never `0.0.0.0/0`, `::/0`, or any CIDR block).\n    - **Egress**: outbound traffic to the AWS control plane and data stores.\n  - `rds`:\n    - **Ingress**: restricted strictly to TCP port `5432` (`from_port = 5432`, `to_port = 5432`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from the `ecs` security group or VPC CIDR (never `0.0.0.0/0`, `::/0`, or `protocol = \"-1\"`).\n    - **Egress**: no outbound egress rules (`egress = []`; passive data stores do not initiate outbound connections, and any out-of-band egress rules on `rds` must be revoked during `deploy.sh` convergence).\n  - `valkey`:\n    - **Ingress**: restricted strictly to TCP port `6379` (`from_port = 6379`, `to_port = 6379`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from the `ecs` security group or VPC CIDR (never `0.0.0.0/0`, `::/0`, or `protocol = \"-1\"`).\n    - **Egress**: no outbound egress rules (`egress = []`; passive data stores do not initiate outbound connections, and any out-of-band egress rules on `valkey` must be revoked during `deploy.sh` convergence).\n- Tag the VPC, subnets, internet gateway, route tables, and security groups with `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>`.\n\n","metadata":{"output":"...\n\ntlement_id>` with a valid TTL in `(0, 90]` and a canonical `SettlementProjection` JSON payload matching PostgreSQL (so `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` immediately after `deploy.sh` hits cache and returns `X-ClearLedger-Source: cache`).\n=== iam.md\n# IAM Roles and Least-Privilege Policies (`services/iam.md`)\n\nProvision six distinct IAM roles (`aws_iam_role`) with role-specific inline or attached policies (`aws_iam_role_policy` or `aws_iam_policy` + `aws_iam_role_policy_attachment`).\n\n## Global Least-Privilege Rules\n\n- Never use wildcard actions (`Action = \"*\"` or `service:*`) or wildcard resources (`Resource = \"*\"`) in any `Allow` statement across any role policy.\n- **Per-workload CloudWatch Log Group isolation**: Each logging workload (`ecs_execution`, `ecs_task`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`) may only grant CloudWatch Logs write permissions (`logs:CreateLogStream`, `logs:PutLogEvents`, `logs:DescribeLogStreams`) on its **own** dedicated CloudWatch Log Group ARN (`arn:aws:logs:<region>:<account>:log-group:/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/<workload>*`). Do not use a shared `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/*` or `/clearledger/*` log group wildcard that permits one workload to write into another workload's log group, and do not grant any CloudWatch Logs actions to `scheduler_role_arn`.\n- **Strict KMS key isolation across all six roles**: Each workload may only be granted `kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, and `kms:DescribeKey` on the specific customer-managed KMS keys required for the data stores it accesses at runtime. No role may grant `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on any of the other KMS keys, and `ecs_execution_role_arn` and `scheduler_role_arn` must not grant access to any of the four KMS keys.\n- **Defense-in-depth explicit `Deny` guardrail statements**: Do not rely solely on implicit omission for forbidden permissions. Every role's policy document must include explicit `Effect = \"Deny\"` statement(s) covering all forbidden actions and resources listed under **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)** for that role below.\n- **Out-of-band IAM policy drift reconciliation in `deploy.sh`**: During `deploy.sh`, any out-of-band inline policies (`DeleteRolePolicy`) or attached customer-managed/AWS-managed policies (`DetachRolePolicy`) added to any of the six ClearLedger IAM roles outside Terraform/OpenTofu must be removed so that each role retains only its canonical least-privilege policy, and any detached out-of-band customer-managed IAM policies prefixed with `<resource_prefix>` must also be deleted (`DeletePolicy`, after deleting any non-default policy versions).\n\n## Role Responsibilities and Access Boundaries\n\n1. **`ecs_execution_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `ecs-tasks.amazonaws.com` (and no other service or wildcard principal).\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**: CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing (`logs:CreateLogStream`, `logs:PutLogEvents`, `logs:DescribeLogStreams`) scoped strictly to `logs.api_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on the audit bucket (`<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`), `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on all four KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on the three non-API CloudWatch Log Groups (`projector`, `relay`, `archiver`).\n2. **`ecs_task_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `ecs-tasks.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Publish-only access on the main SQS queue ARN (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:GetQueueAttributes`, `sqs:GetQueueUrl`).\n     - Read-only access on the projection DynamoDB table ARN and its specific `AccountIndex` GSI ARN `<table_arn>/index/AccountIndex` (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:Query`, `dynamodb:DescribeTable`; do not use `<table_arn>/index/*` or `<table_arn>/*` wildcards).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `messaging` and `projection` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.api_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, consuming or deleting SQS messages (`sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or `sqs:SendMessage`/`sqs:ReceiveMessage`/`sqs:DeleteMessage` on the DLQ; mutating DynamoDB (`dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteTable`) on the projection table; any S3 access (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`; `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `database` and `audit` KMS keys; and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-API log group (`projector`, `relay`, `archiver`). Furthermore, must not grant `dynamodb:Query` on `<table_arn>/index/*`.\n3. **`projector_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `lambda.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Consume-only access on the main SQS queue ARN (`sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`, `sqs:GetQueueAttributes`, `sqs:ChangeMessageVisibility`) and dead-letter forwarding (`sqs:SendMessage`) on the DLQ ARN.\n     - Append/monotonic-upsert access on the projection DynamoDB table ARN and its specific `AccountIndex` GSI ARN `<table_arn>/index/AccountIndex` (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:Query`; do not use `<table_arn>/index/*` or `<table_arn>/*` wildcards).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `messaging` and `projection` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.projector_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, `sqs:SendMessage` on the main queue or `sqs:ReceiveMessage`/`sqs:DeleteMessage` on the DLQ; deleting DynamoDB items (`dynamodb:DeleteItem`) or tables (`dynamodb:DeleteTable`) on the projection table; any S3 access (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`; `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `database` and `audit` KMS keys; and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-projector log group (`api`, `relay`, `archiver`). Furthermore, must not grant `dynamodb:Query` on `<table_arn>/index/*`.\n4. **`relay_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `lambda.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Publish-only access on the main SQS queue ARN (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:GetQueueAttributes`, `sqs:GetQueueUrl`).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `messaging` and `database` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.relay_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage` or `sqs:DeleteMessage` on the main queue, `sqs:SendMessage`/`sqs:ReceiveMessage`/`sqs:DeleteMessage` on the DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`, `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `projection` and `audit` KMS keys, and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-relay log group (`api`, `projector`, `archiver`).\n5. **`archiver_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `lambda.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Append-only object access (`s3:PutObject`, `s3:GetObject`, `s3:AbortMultipartUpload`) scoped strictly to `<audit_bucket_arn>/ledger-audit/*`, and bucket-level metadata (`s3:ListBucket`, `s3:GetBucketLocation`) scoped strictly to `<audit_bucket_arn>` (keep object-level and bucket-level statements separated by resource type).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `audit` and `database` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.archiver_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not grant `s3:PutObject` on the bucket ARN `<audit_bucket_arn>` or unscoped `<audit_bucket_arn>/*` root wildcard, and must not grant `s3:ListBucket` on object ARNs. Must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"` `s3:DeleteObject` and `s3:DeleteObjectVersion` on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`, any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `messaging` and `projection` KMS keys, and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-archiver log group (`api`, `projector`, `relay`).\n6. **`scheduler_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `scheduler.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - `lambda:InvokeFunction` scoped strictly to the Outbox Relay (`workers.outbox_relay.function_arn`) and Audit Archiver (`workers.audit_archiver.function_arn`) Lambda function ARNs.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, `lambda:InvokeFunction` on the Projector Lambda (`workers.projector.function_arn`), any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`, `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on all four KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on all four CloudWatch Log Groups (`api`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`).\n=== kms.md\n# Customer-Managed KMS Keys (`services/kms.md`)\n\nProvision four separate customer-managed KMS keys (`aws_kms_key`) and four KMS aliases (`aws_kms_alias`):\n\n- `kms.database_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-database`: encrypts the RDS PostgreSQL instance\n- `kms.messaging_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-messaging`: encrypts the main SQS queue and DLQ\n- `kms.projection_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-projection`: encrypts the DynamoDB projection table\n- `kms.audit_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-audit`: encrypts the S3 audit archive bucket\n\nEach of the four KMS keys must be configured with:\n- `enable_key_rotation = true`\n- `deletion_window_in_days = 10` (must be `10..30` days)\n- Tag `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>`\n\n=== lambda.md\n# Lambda Workers and Event Source Mapping (`services/lambda.md`)\n\nProvision three container-based AWS Lambda functions (`aws_lambda_function`, `package_type = \"Image\"`):\n\n1. **Projector (`workers.projector`)**:\n   - `image_uri = projector_image` from `config.json`\n   - `role = iam.projector_role_arn`\n   - Environment variables: `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`, `PROJECTION_TABLE`, `VALKEY_URL`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`\n   - Wired to the main SQS queue via an `aws_lambda_event_source_mapping` with:\n     - `enabled = true`\n     - `batch_size = 5`\n     - `maximum_batching_window_in_seconds = 0`\n     - `function_response_types = [\"ReportBatchItemFailures\"]`\n2. **Outbox Relay (`workers.outbox_relay`)**:\n   - `image_uri = relay_image` from `config.json`\n   - `role = iam.relay_role_arn`\n   - Environment variables: `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`, `DATABASE_URL`, `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE = \"50\"`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`\n3. **Audit Archiver (`workers.audit_archiver`)**:\n   - `image_uri = archiver_image` from `config.json`\n   - `role = iam.archiver_role_arn`\n   - Environment variables: `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`, `DATABASE_URL`, `AUDIT_BUCKET`, `AUDIT_PREFIX = \"ledger-audit/\"`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`\n=== rds.md\n# RDS PostgreSQL (`services/rds.md`)\n\n- Provision a DB subnet group (`aws_db_subnet_group`) spanning strictly the private subnets (`network.private_subnet_ids`).\n- Provision a single PostgreSQL 16 RDS instance (`aws_db_instance`) with:\n  - `engine = \"postgres\"`\n  - `engine_version = \"16.3\"` (or `16.*`)\n  - `instance_class = \"db.t4g.micro\"`\n  - `db_name`, `username`, and `password` from `/workspace/config/config.json`\n  - `storage_encrypted = true`\n  - `kms_key_id` set to the customer-managed `database` KMS key ARN (`kms.database_arn`)\n  - `publicly_accessible = false`\n  - `skip_final_snapshot = true`\n  - `vpc_security_group_ids` containing the `rds` security group\n- Re-running `deploy.sh` must never replace the RDS instance or lose committed data.\n\n## Required Database Schema, Relational Invariants, and Triggers\n\nThe pre-built Rust binaries (`clearledger-api`, `clearledger-outbox-relay`, and `clearledger-audit-archiver`) do **not** run database migrations on startup. `clearledger-api` relies on PostgreSQL `CHECK`/`UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY` constraints and PL/pgSQL triggers to enforce domain, schema, and state-transition invariants at the database layer (mapping SQLSTATE `23514`, `23503`, `23502`, `23505`, and `P0001` exceptions to HTTP `400 Bad Request`), and `GET /health/ready` verifies that all four tables, all three indexes, and user-defined triggers on all four tables exist in the `clearledger` schema before reporting `checks.postgres = \"UP\"`.\n\n`deploy.sh` must idempotently initialize the `clearledger` schema, all four tables, their constraints, their triggers, and all three indexes on the RDS PostgreSQL instance.\n\n### 1. Table `clearledger.settlements`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `account_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `reference TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `debit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `credit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_status TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_stage TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `last_entry_id UUID NULL`\n  - `last_memo TEXT NULL`\n  - `version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE UPDATE` trigger)**:\n  - Trimmed string length bounds and allowed status values must match `CreateSettlementRequest` and `AppendEntryRequest` in `openapi.yaml` (including `1..256` trimmed characters when `last_memo` is non-null), with trimmed `debit_party <> credit_party`, `version >= 1`, and monotonic timestamps (`updated_at >= created_at` on insert and `NEW.updated_at >= OLD.updated_at` on update).\n  - At initiation (`version = 1`), `entry_count = 0`, `current_status = 'INITIATED'`, `last_entry_id IS NULL`, and `updated_at = created_at`. For `version > 1`, `entry_count = version - 1`, `current_status <> 'INITIATED'`, and `last_entry_id IS NOT NULL`.\n  - On `UPDATE`: header columns (`settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `created_at`) are immutable; each update must step `version` and `entry_count` by `+1` with a new `last_entry_id` (`NEW.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id`); and `current_status` must follow the clearing lifecycle progression:\n    - The non-`DISPUTED` clearing status rank order is `INITIATED` (`0`) < `VALIDATED` (`1`) < `RESERVED` (`2`) < `CLEARED` (`3`) < `SETTLED` (`4`) < `RECONCILED` (`5`).\n    - `RECONCILED` is strictly terminal: once `OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`, any `UPDATE` on the settlement row must be rejected (even if `NEW.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`).\n    - From any non-`RECONCILED`, non-`DISPUTED` status (`INITIATED`, `VALIDATED`, `RESERVED`, `CLEARED`, `SETTLED`), `NEW.current_status` may transition to `'DISPUTED'` or to any status whose rank is monotonic non-decreasing (`rank(NEW.current_status) >= rank(OLD.current_status)` — permitting same-status updates such as `CLEARED` -> `CLEARED` and single- or multi-stage forward advances such as `INITIATED` -> `VALIDATED` or `INITIATED` -> `CLEARED`, while rejecting any backward rank regression where `rank(NEW.current_status) < rank(OLD.current_status)` such as `CLEARED` -> `RESERVED`).\n    - From `OLD.current_status = 'DISPUTED'`, `NEW.current_status` may only remain `'DISPUTED'` or resolve to terminal `'RECONCILED'`.\n\n### 2. Table `clearledger.events`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `event_type TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `occurred_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE INSERT`/`UPDATE`/`DELETE` triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `UNIQUE (settlement_id, idempotency_key)`, with trimmed length bounds on `correlation_id` and `idempotency_key` matching `openapi.yaml`.\n  - `payload` must conform strictly to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` in `schemas/events.schema.json` (including `additionalProperties: false` on both the envelope and `payload.data`, required non-null `accountId`, `reference`, `debitParty`, `creditParty`, `status`, and `clearingStage` in `payload.data` with trimmed string length bounds matching `openapi.yaml` and `1..256` trimmed characters when `data.memo` is non-null, column-to-envelope equality across `event_id`, `settlement_id`, `aggregate_version`, `event_type`, `correlation_id`, `idempotency_key`, and `occurred_at` compared as `TIMESTAMPTZ`, and version/kind/status/entryId coupling for `SettlementInitiated` at `v = 1` vs `LedgerEntryRecorded` at `v >= 2`).\n  - On `INSERT`: per-settlement `aggregate_version` must be contiguous starting at `1`; `data.entryId` must be unique per settlement across all `LedgerEntryRecorded` events; the event must match the parent row in `clearledger.settlements` (including header fields, `current_status`, `current_stage`, `last_entry_id`, `last_memo`, `version`, and `occurred_at = updated_at`, plus `occurred_at = created_at` at `v = 1`); and for `aggregate_version >= 2`, `NEW.occurred_at` must be `>=` the immediately preceding event's `occurred_at` (`aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1`) and the status transition from that preceding event's `data.status` to `NEW.payload->'data'->>'status'` must satisfy the settlement status transition rules above (monotonic non-decreasing rank, reachable `DISPUTED` from non-`RECONCILED`, `DISPUTED` only to `DISPUTED` or `RECONCILED`, and no transitions out of or repeating `RECONCILED`).\n  - `clearledger.events` is strictly append-only (`UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected).\n\n### 3. Table `clearledger.outbox`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES clearledger.events(event_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `published_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `archived_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `last_error TEXT NULL`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY`/`CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `FOREIGN KEY (settlement_id, aggregate_version) REFERENCES clearledger.events(settlement_id, aggregate_version) ON DELETE CASCADE`.\n  - `payload` must conform to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` (`schemas/events.schema.json`) and on `INSERT` must exactly mirror the corresponding row in `clearledger.events` in contiguous per-settlement `aggregate_version` order starting at `1`.\n  - Delivery/archival lifecycle: `attempts >= 0`; when `published_at IS NOT NULL`, `attempts >= 1`, `last_error IS NULL`, and `published_at >= created_at`; `archived_at` requires `published_at IS NOT NULL` and `archived_at >= published_at`.\n  - `DELETE` is forbidden; on `UPDATE`, envelope columns are immutable, `attempts` cannot decrease, publishing an unpublished row requires incrementing `attempts` with `archived_at IS NULL`, while `published_at` remains `NOT NULL` (such as during archival or resetting `archived_at = NULL` for S3 re-archival), `published_at`, `attempts`, and `last_error` cannot be mutated (resetting `published_at = NULL, archived_at = NULL` for operational replay is permitted), and once `OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL` and `NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL`, `NEW.archived_at` cannot be mutated to a different timestamp without first resetting `archived_at = NULL`.\n\n### 4. Table `clearledger.idempotency_keys`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `scope TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `request_hash TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `status_code INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `response_body JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - Composite `PRIMARY KEY (scope, idempotency_key)`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - `scope` (`create:<settlement_uuid>` or `entry:<settlement_uuid>`), `idempotency_key` (`8..128` trimmed chars), lowercase hex SHA-256 `request_hash`, `status_code` (`201` for `create:*` at `version = 1`; `202` for `entry:*` at `version >= 2`), and closed-schema `response_body` (`WriteAcceptedResponse` in `openapi.yaml` with `accepted = true` and `idempotentReplay = false`).\n  - On `INSERT`, the referenced event (`eventId`, `settlementId`, `version`, `idempotency_key`) must exist in both `clearledger.events` and `clearledger.outbox`. `UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected.\n\n### 5. Required Indexes in `clearledger` Schema\n\n- `idx_clearledger_outbox_unpublished`: on `clearledger.outbox (seq)` with partial predicate `WHERE published_at IS NULL`\n- `idx_clearledger_outbox_unarchived`: on `clearledger.outbox (seq)` with partial predicate `WHERE published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`\n- `idx_clearledger_events_settlement_version`: on `clearledger.events (settlement_id, aggregate_version)`\n=== s3.md\n# S3 Audit Archive (`services/s3.md`)\n\n- Provision a dedicated S3 bucket (`aws_s3_bucket`) for NDJSON audit batches (`force_destroy = true` recommended so `destroy.sh` can remove the bucket after versioned audit objects and delete markers are written).\n- Enable bucket versioning (`aws_s3_bucket_versioning` with `status = \"Enabled\"`).\n- Enable default server-side encryption (`aws_s3_bucket_server_side_encryption_configuration`) using `sse_algorithm = \"aws:kms\"` and `kms_master_key_id = kms.audit_arn`.\n- Block all public access (`aws_s3_bucket_public_access_block`) with all four flags (`block_public_acls`, `block_public_policy`, `ignore_public_acls`, `restrict_public_buckets`) set to `true`.\n- Configure the Audit Archiver Lambda with `AUDIT_BUCKET = <bucket_name>` and `AUDIT_PREFIX = \"ledger-audit/\"` (`manifest.audit.prefix = \"ledger-audit/\"`).\n- `clearledger-audit-archiver` writes NDJSON batches to `s3://<bucket_name>/ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`, where `<first_seq:08d>` and `<last_seq:08d>` are the 8-digit zero-padded first and last `clearledger.outbox.seq` in the batch, each batch contains a contiguous, gap-free slice of all committed `clearledger.outbox` rows whose `seq` lies in `[first_seq, last_seq]` ordered by strictly ascending `clearledger.outbox.seq`, batch `[first_seq, last_seq]` intervals are strictly disjoint and non-overlapping across objects, and `<16_char_sha256_hex>` is the first 16 lowercase hexadecimal characters of the SHA-256 digest of the object's raw bytes.\n- During `deploy.sh` data-plane convergence, reconcile the versioned S3 audit bucket against `clearledger.outbox` so that all objects reside under `ledger-audit/`, every batch object has a valid canonical key (`batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`), valid SHA-256 digest, contiguous gap-free `clearledger.outbox.seq` coverage across `[first_seq, last_seq]`, and non-overlapping `[first_seq, last_seq]` bounds across batches, every committed event in `clearledger.outbox` appears **exactly once** across the S3 audit batches with its exact canonical JSON `payload` and `archived_at IS NOT NULL` in PostgreSQL, and all noncurrent object `Versions` (`IsLatest = false`) and `DeleteMarkers` (`ListObjectVersions` / `DeleteObject` with `VersionId`) are permanently purged from the bucket so only the single current version (`IsLatest = true`) of each valid canonical batch remains.\n\n=== (`aws_security_group`, four distinct security group IDs) enforcing least-privilege ingress and egress:\n  - `alb`:\n    - **Ingress**: TCP port `80` (`from_port = 80`, `to_port = 80`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from `0.0.0.0/0`.\n    - **Egress**: restricted strictly to TCP port `8080` (`from_port = 8080`, `to_port = 8080`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) targeting the `ecs` security group or VPC CIDR (must restricted strictly to TCP port `5432` (`from_port = 5432`, `to_port = 5432`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from the `ecs` security group or VPC CIDR (never `0.0.0.0/0`, `::/0`, or `protocol = \"-1\"`).\n    - **Egress**: no outbound egress rules (`egress = []`; passive data stores do not initiate outbound connections, and any out-of-band egress rules on `rds` must be revoked during `deploy.sh` convergence).\n  - `valkey`:\n    - **Ingress**: restricted strictly to TCP port `6379` (`from_port = 6379`, `to_port = 6379`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from the `ecs` security group or VPC CIDR (never `0.0.0.0/0`, `::/0`, or `protocol = \"-1\"`).\n    - **Egress**: no outbound egress rules (`egress = []`; passive data stores do not initiate outbound connections, and any out-of-band egress rules on `valkey` must be revoked during `deploy.sh` convergence).\n- Tag the VPC, subnets, internet gateway, route tables, and security groups with `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>`.\n\n","exit":0,"truncated":false},"title":"cd /workspace/contracts/services && for f in *.md; do echo \"=== $f\"; cat $f; WHEN 'INITIATED'  THEN 0\n    WHEN 'VALIDATED'  THEN 1\n    WHEN 'RESERVED'   THEN 2\n    WHEN 'CLEARED'    THEN 3\n    WHEN 'SETTLED'    THEN 4\n    WHEN 'RECONCILED' THEN 5\n    ELSE NULL\n  END\n$$;\n\n-- Clearing lifecycle transition rule shared by settlements and events.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.valid_transition(old_status text, new_status text)\nRETURNS boolean LANGUAGE ('DISPUTED', 'RECONCILED')\n    WHEN new_status = 'DISPUTED' THEN clearledger.status_rank(old_status) IS NOT NULL\n    WHEN clearledger.status_rank(old_status) IS NULL\n      OR clearledger.status_rank(new_status) IS NULL THEN false\n    ELSE clearledger.status_rank(new_status) >= clearledger.status_rank(old_status)\n  END\n$$;\n\n-- Strict validation of ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope (events.schema.json).\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.valid_envelope(p jsonb)\nRETURNS boolean LANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS $$\nDECLARE\n  d jsonb;\n  k text;\n  v numeric;\n  ts_re constant text :=\n    '^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}[Tt][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]+)?([Zz]|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$';\nBEGIN\n  IF p IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(p) <> 'object' clearledger.is_uuid_text(p->>'eventId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'eventType') <> 'string'\n     OR p->>'eventType' NOT IN ('SettlementInitiated', 'LedgerEntryRecorded') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  d := p->'data';\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  FOR k IN SELECT jsonb_object_keys(d) LOOP\n    IF k NOT IN ('kind', 'accountId', 'reference', others THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- Adds a named constraint only when it does not already exist.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.ensure_constraint(tbl regclass, cname text, ddl text)\nRETURNS void LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint WHERE conrelid = tbl AND conname = cname) THEN\n    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s ADD CONSTRAINT %I $c$CHECK (clearledger.tlen(idempotency_key) BETWEEN 8 AND 128)$c$);\nSELECT clearledger.ensure_constraint('clearledger.events', 'events_payload_envelope_chk',\n  $c$CHECK ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nSELECT clearledger.ensure_constraint('clearledger.idempotency_keys', 'idempotency_keys_scope_chk',\n  $c$CHECK (scope ~* '^(create|entry):[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$')$c$);\nSELECT clearledger.ensure_constraint('clearledger.idempotency_keys', 'idempotency_keys_key_len_chk',\n  $c$CHECK idx_clearledger_events_settlement_version\n  ON clearledger.events (settlement_id, aggregate_version);\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Trigger functions\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n-- settlements: header immutability, +1 stepping and lifecycle progression.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_settlements_before_update()\nRETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nBEGIN\n  IF OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % is RECONCILED (terminal) and cannot be modified', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.settlement_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.settlement_id\n     OR NEW.account_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.account_id\n     OR NEW.reference IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.reference\n     OR NEW.debit_party IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.debit_party\n     OR NEW.credit_party IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.credit_party\n     OR NEW.created_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.created_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement header columns are immutable';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.version IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.version + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement version must step by +1 (old %, new %)', OLD.version, NEW.version;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.entry_count IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.entry_count + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement entry_count must step by +1';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.last_entry_id IS NULL OR NEW.last_entry_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement update requires a new last_entry_id';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.updated_at < OLD.updated_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement updated_at must be monotonic';\n  END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.valid_transition(OLD.current_status, NEW.current_status) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid settlement status transition % -> %', OLD.current_status, NEW.current_status;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- events: contiguity, entryId uniqueness, parent consistency, ordering.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_events_before_insert()\nRETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nDECLARE\n  s clearledger.settlements%ROWTYPE;\n  d jsonb;\n  max_version integer;\n  prev clearledger.events%ROWTYPE;\n  new_entry text;\n  new_memo text;\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT clearledger.event_matches_envelope(NEW.payload, NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id, NEW.aggregate_version,\n                                            NEW.event_type, NEW.correlation_id, NEW.idempotency_key, NEW.occurred_at) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event payload does not conform to ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope or mismatches columns'\n      USING ERRCODE = '23514';\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT * INTO s FROM clearledger.settlements WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % does not exist', NEW.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT max(aggregate_version) INTO max_version FROM clearledger.events WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version IS DISTINCT FROM coalesce(max_version, 0) + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event aggregate_version % is not contiguous (expected %)',\n      NEW.aggregate_version, coalesce(max_version, 0) + 1;\n  END IF;\n\n  d := NEW.payload->'data';\n  new_entry := CASE WHEN jsonb_typeof(d->'entryId') = 'string' THEN d->>'entryId' END;\n  new_memo := CASE WHEN jsonb_typeof(d->'memo') = 'string' THEN d->>'memo' END;\n\n  IF new_entry IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS (\n      SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.events e\n       WHERE e.settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id\n         AND e.event_type = 'LedgerEntryRecorded'\n         AND jsonb_typeof(e.payload->'data'->'entryId') = 'string'\n         AND (e.payload->'data'->>'entryId')::uuid = new_entry::uuid) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'entryId % already recorded for settlement %', new_entry, NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF d->>'accountId' IS DISTINCT FROM s.account_id\n     OR d->>'reference' IS DISTINCT FROM s.reference\n     OR d->>'debitParty' IS DISTINCT FROM s.debit_party\n     OR d->>'creditParty' IS DISTINCT FROM s.credit_party\n     OR d->>'status' IS DISTINCT FROM s.current_status\n     OR d->>'clearingStage' IS DISTINCT FROM s.current_stage\n     OR new_entry::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM s.last_entry_id\n     OR new_memo IS DISTINCT FROM s.last_memo\n     OR NEW.aggregate_version IS DISTINCT FROM s.version\n     OR NEW.occurred_at IS DISTINCT FROM s.updated_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event does not match settlement % state', NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version = 1 THEN\n    IF NEW.occurred_at IS DISTINCT FROM s.created_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'initiation event occurred_at must equal settlement created_at';\n    END IF;\n  ELSE\n    SELECT * INTO prev FROM clearledger.events\n     WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id AND aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1;\n    IF NOT FOUND THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'previous event missing for settlement %', NEW.settlement_id;\n    END IF;\n    IF NEW.occurred_at < prev.occurred_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'event occurred_at must not precede the previous event';\n    END IF;\n    IF NOT clearledger.valid_transition(prev.payload->'data'->>'status', d->>'status') THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid event status transition % -> %', prev.payload->'data'->>'status', d->>'status';\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_append_only()\nRETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nBEGIN\n  RAISE EXCEPTION '%.% is append-only: % is not permitted', TG_TABLE_SCHEMA, TG_TABLE_NAME, TG_OP;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- outbox: must mirror events, contiguous per settlement.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_outbox_before_insert()\nRETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nDECLARE\n  e clearledger.events%ROWTYPE;\n  max_version integer;\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT clearledger.outbox_matches_envelope(NEW.payload, NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id,\n                                             NEW.aggregate_version, NEW.correlation_id) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox payload does not conform to ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope or mismatches columns'\n      USING ERRCODE = '23514';\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT * INTO e FROM clearledger.events WHERE event_id = NEW.event_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox event % does not exist in clearledger.events', NEW.event_id USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n  IF e.settlement_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.settlement_id\n     OR e.aggregate_version IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.aggregate_version\n     OR e.correlation_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.correlation_id\n     OR e.payload IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.payload THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox row must exactly mirror clearledger.events row %', NEW.event_id;\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT max(aggregate_version) INTO max_version FROM clearledger.outbox WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version IS DISTINCT FROM coalesce(max_version, 0) + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox aggregate_version % is not contiguous (expected %)',\n      NEW.aggregate_version, coalesce(max_version, 0) + 1;\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- outbox: delivery / archival lifecycle.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_outbox_before_update()\nRETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nBEGIN\n  IF NEW.seq IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.seq\n     OR NEW.event_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.event_id\n     OR NEW.settlement_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.settlement_id\n     OR NEW.aggregate_version IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.aggregate_version\n     OR NEW.correlation_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.correlation_id\n     OR NEW.payload IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.payload\n     OR NEW.created_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.created_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox envelope columns are immutable';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NEW.attempts < OLD.attempts THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox attempts cannot decrease';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF OLD.published_at IS NULL AND NEW.published_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n    -- publishing an unpublished row\n    IF NEW.attempts <= OLD.attempts THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'publishing an outbox row requires incrementing attempts';\n    END IF;\n    IF NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'an outbox row cannot be archived in the same step it is published';\n    END IF;\n  ELSIF OLD.published_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.published_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n    -- archival / re-archival of a published row\n    IF NEW.published_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.published_at\n       OR NEW.attempts IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.attempts\n       OR NEW.last_error IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_error THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'published_at, attempts and last_error are immutable once published';\n    END IF;\n    IF OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL\n       AND NEW.archived_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.archived_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'archived_at cannot be changed without first resetting it to NULL';\n    END IF;\n  ELSIF OLD.published_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.published_at IS NULL THEN\n    -- operational replay reset\n    IF NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'replay reset requires archived_at = NULL';\n    END IF;\n  ELSE\n    -- still unpublished (e.g. failed attempt bookkeeping)\n    IF NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'unpublished outbox rows cannot be archived';\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- idempotency_keys: referenced event must exist in events and outbox.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_idempotency_before_insert()\nRETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nDECLARE\n  b jsonb := NEW.response_body;\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT clearledger.valid_write_response(NEW.scope, NEW.status_code, b) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'idempotency response_body is not a valid WriteAcceptedResponse' USING         AND e.aggregate_version = (b->>'version')::integer\n         AND e.idempotency_key = NEW.idempotency_key) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'idempotency record references an unknown event' USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n  IF NOT EXISTS (\n      SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.outbox o\n       WHERE o.event_id = (b->>'eventId')::uuid\n         AND o.settlement_id = (b->>'settlementId')::uuid\n         AND o.aggregate_version = (b->>'version')::integer) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'idempotency record references an event missing from the outbox' USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Triggers\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER trg_settlements_before_update\n  BEFORE UPDATE ON clearledger.settlements\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_settlements_before_update();\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_outbox_before_insert();\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER trg_outbox_before_update\n  BEFORE UPDATE ON clearledger.outbox\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_append_only();\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER trg_idempotency_before_insert\n  BEFORE INSERT ON clearledger.idempotency_keys\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION 8080\n            ranges = [r.get(\"CidrIp\") for r in perm.get(\"IpRanges\", [])]\n            v6 = perm.get(\"Ipv6Ranges\", [])\n            if not ok_port or v6 or any(r in (\"0.0.0.0/0\",) for r   log(f\"revoking {len(bad)} out-of-band egress rule(s) on {role} security group\")\n            for perm in bad:\n                clean = {k: v for k, v in perm.items() if k in\n                         (\"IpProtocol\", \"FromPort\", \"ToPort\", \"IpRanges\", \"Ipv6Ranges\",\n                          \"UserIdGroupPairs\", \"PrefixListIds\") and v not in (None, [])}\n ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef cmd_esm_check(state_path):\n    \"\"\"Prints 'replace' when the projector ESM must be re-created/re-bound.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        with open(state_path) as fh:\n            st = json.load(fh)\n    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n        return\n    esm_uuid, queue_name = None, None\n    for r in   if r.get(\"type\") == \"aws_sqs_queue\" and r.get(\"name\") == \"main\" and r.get(\"instances\"):\n            queue_name = r[\"instances\"][0][\"attributes\"].get(\"name\")\n    if not esm_uuid or ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Data-plane reconciliation\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef invoke(fn_name, payload=None):\n    lam = aws(\"lambda\")\n    r = lam.invoke(FunctionName=fn_name, InvocationType=\"RequestResponse\",\n                   Payload=json.dumps(payload or {}).encode())\n    body = r[\"Payload\"].read().decode(errors=\"replace\")\n    if r.get(\"FunctionError\"):\n        log(f\"  {fn_name} returned error: {body[:300]}\")\n    return body\n\n\ndef drain_outbox(pg, m):\n    fn = m[\"workers\"][\"outbox_relay\"][\"function_name\"]\n    for i in range(60):\n        n = int(pg.run(\"SELECT count(*) FROM clearledger.outbox WHERE published_at IS NULL\").strip())\n        if n == 0:\n            log(\"outbox drained (0 unpublished rows)\")\n            return\n        log(f\"outbox has {n} unpublished row(s); invoking {fn}\")\n        safe(invoke, fn)\n        time.sleep(0.5 if i < 10 else 2)\n    log(\"WARNING: outbox still has unpublished rows after relay attempts\")\n\n\ndef queue_depth(url):\n    a = \"ApproximateNumberOfMessagesDelayed\"])[\"Attributes\"]\n    return sum(int(a.get(k, 0)) for k in a)\n\n\ndef wait_queue_idle(m, timeout=150):\n    url = m[\"messaging\"][\"queue_url\"]\n    deadline = time.time() + timeout\n    zero_streak = 0\n    while time.time() < deadline:\n        try:\n            d = queue_depth(url)\n        except ClientError as e:\n            log(f\"queue depth error {err_code(e)}\")\n            return\n        if d == 0:\n            zero_streak += 1\n            if zero_streak >= 2:\n                log(\"main queue idle\")\n                return\n        else:\n            zero_streak = 0\n        time.sleep(2)\n    log(\"WARNING: main queue not idle before timeout; continuing\")\n\n\ndef ddb_attr(v):\n    if isinstance(v, bool):\n        return {\"BOOL\": v}\n    if isinstance(v, int):\n        return {\"N\": str(v)}\n    return {\"S\": str(v)}\n\n\ndef expected_projection(pg):\n    settlements = pg.json(\n        \"SELECT      + ts_cols(\"updated_at\", \"upd\") + \" FROM clearledger.settlements ORDER BY settlement_id\")\n    events = pg.json(\n        \"SELECT settlement_id::text AS sid, event_id::text AS event_id, expected = {}\n    for s in settlements:\n        pk = f\"SETTLEMENT#{s['sid']}\"\n        item = {\n            \"PK\": pk, \"SK\": \"STATE\",\n            \"GSI1PK\": f\"ACCOUNT#{s['account_id']}\", item[\"memo\"] = d[\"memo\"]\n        expected[(pk, sk)] = item\n    return settlements, expected\n\n\ndef item_matches(actual, exp):\n    if set(actual.keys()) != set(exp.keys()):\n        return False\n    for k, v in exp.items():\n        a = actual[k]\n        if k == \"envelope\":\n            if \"S\" not in a:\n                return False\n            try:\n                if json.loads(a[\"S\"]) != json.loads(v):\n                    return False\n            except ValueError:\n                return False\n            continue\n        if a != ddb_attr(v):\n            return False\n    return True\n\n\ndef reconcile_dynamodb(pg, m):\n    table = m[\"projections\"][\"table_name\"]\n    ddb = aws(\"dynamodb\")\n    settlements, expected = expected_projection(pg)\n    actual = {}\n    for page in ddb.get_paginator(\"scan\").paginate(TableName=table, ConsistentRead=True):\n        for it in page.get(\"Items\", []):\n            pk = it.get(\"PK\", {}).get(\"S\")\n            sk = it.get(\"SK\", {}).get(\"S\")\n            actual[(pk, sk)] = it\n    puts, deletes = [], []\n    for key, exp in expected.items():\n        a = actual.get(key)\n        if a is None or [{\"PutRequest\": {\"Item\": p}} for p in puts] + [{\"DeleteRequest\": {\"Key\": d}} for d in deletes]\n    for i in range(0, len(reqs), 25):\n        chunk = {table: reqs[i:i + 25]}\n        for _ in
